(CHAPTER 252) MUNICIPAL COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under sections 2, 5 and 10 of the Municipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252) to beread with Section 18 of the Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councilsand Local Government, do by this order, further amend the order published in the Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of10th August, 1978 relating to the Colombo Municipal Council as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Colombo Municipal Council shall consist of such number of members to be elected andreturned in terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i.Sixty Six members to be elected under paragraph (a) of subsection (1) of section 5 of the Municipal CouncilsOrdinance (Chapter 252) ;

ii.Forty Four members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of subsection (1) of section 5 of theMunicipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252) ;

iii.Such number of members determined by the Commissioner of Elections under paragraph (a) subsection (4)of Section 65B of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) in terms of paragraph (c) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of the Municipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252)”

2. The term of office of the members of this Municipal Council shall commence with effect from 15th February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,01st November, 2017.

(CHAPTER 252) MUNICIPAL COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under sections 2, 5 and 10 of the Municipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252) to beread with Section 18 of the Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councilsand Local Government, do by this order, further amend the order published in the Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of10th August, 1978 relating to the Dehiwala-Mt. Lavinia Municipal Council as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Dehiwala-Mt. Lavinia Municipal Council shall consist of such number of members to beelected and returned in terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i.Twenty Nine members to be elected under paragraph (a) of subsection (1) of section 5 of the MunicipalCouncils Ordinance (Chapter 252) ;

ii.Nineteen members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of subsection (1) of section 5 of theMunicipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252) ;

iii.Such number of members determined by the Commissioner of Elections under paragraph (a) subsection (4)of Section 65B of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) in terms of paragraph (c) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of the Municipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252)”

2. The term of office of the members of this Municipal Council shall commence with effect from 15th February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,01st November, 2017.

PRADESHIYA SABHAS ACT, No. 15 OF 1987

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under Sub-section (1) of Section 2, Section 4 and Section 6 of the PradeshiyaSabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987 to be read with Section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, further amend the order, published in the GazetteExtraordinary No. 453/5 of 12th May, 1987 relating to the Seethawaka Pradeshiya Sabha as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Seethawaka Pradeshiya Sabha shall consist of such number of members to be electedand returned in terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i.Twenty Six members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of section 4 of the Pradeshiya SabhasAct, No. 15 of 1987;

ii.Seventeen members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of thePradeshiya Sabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987;

(CHAPTER 255) URBAN COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under sections 2, 5 and 10 of Urban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255) to be read withSection 18 of the Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councils and LocalGovernment, do by this order, further amend the order, published in the Gazette Extraordinary No. 231/13 of 09thFebruary, 1983 relating to the Peliyagoda Urban Council as follows:

1. By the amendment of the number of members mentioned in the Schedule thereof, follows :

“(4) Determine that the Peliyagoda Urban Council shall consist of such number of members to be elected and returnedin terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

(CHAPTER 255) URBAN COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under sections 2, 5 and 10 of the Urban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255) to be readwith Section 18 of the Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councils andLocal Government, do by this order, further amend the order published in the Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of 10thAugust, 1978 relating to the Horana Urban Council as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Horana Urban Council shall consist of such number of members to be elected and returned interms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i.Seven members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of section 5 of the Urban CouncilsOrdinance (Chapter 255) ;

ii.Four members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of the UrbanCouncils Ordinance (Chapter 255) ;

iii.Such number of members determined by the Commissioner of Elections under paragraph (a) of Sub-section(4) of Section 65B of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) in terms of paragraph (c) ofSub-section (1) of Section 5 of the Urban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255)”

2. The term of office of the members of this Urban Council shall commence with effect from 15th February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,01st November, 2017.

2. The term of office of the members of this Pradeshiya Sabha shall commence with effect from 15th February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,01st November, 2017.

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under Sub-section (1) of Section 2, Section 4 and Section 6 of the PradeshiyaSabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987 to be read with Section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, further amend the order, published in the GazetteExtraordinary No. 453/11 of 12th May, 1987 relating to the Neluwa Pradeshiya Sabha as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Neluwa Pradeshiya Sabha shall consist of such number of members to be elected and returnedin terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i.Ten members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the Pradeshiya Sabhas Act,No. 15 of 1987;

I fldgi ( ^I& fPoh - YS% ,xld m%cd;dka;s%l iudcjd§ ckrcfha w;s úfYI .eiÜ m;%h - 2017'11'02PART I : SEC. (I) - GAZETTE EXTRAORDINARY OF THE DEMOCRATIC SOCIALIST REPUBLIC OF SRI LANKA - 02.11.201766A

ii.Six members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the PradeshiyaSabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987;

iii.Such number of members determined by the Commissioner of Elections under paragraph (a) Sub-section(4) of Section 65B of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) in terms of paragraph (b) ofSub-section (1) of Section 4 of the Pradeshiya Sabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987”

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under Sub-section (1) of Section 2, Section 4 and Section 6 of the PradeshiyaSabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987 to be read with Section 18 of the Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, further amend the order, which was finallyamended by the Gazette Extraordinary No. 1421/26 of 01st December, 2005, published in the Gazette ExtraordinaryNo. 453/12 of 12th May, 1987 relating to the Hakmana Pradeshiya Sabha as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Hakmana Pradeshiya Sabha shall consist of such number of members to be elected andreturned in terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i.Ten members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the Pradeshiya Sabhas Act,No. 15 of 1987;

ii.Six members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the PradeshiyaSabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987;

iii.Such number of members determined by the Commissioner of Elections under paragraph (a) Sub-section (4)of Section 65B of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) in terms of paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the Pradeshiya Sabhas Act No. 15 of 1987”

2. The term of office of the members of this Pradeshiya Sabha shall commence with effect from 15th February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,01st November, 2017.

2. The term of Office of the members of this Pradeshiya Sabha shall commence with effect from 15th of February, 2018.

(CHAPTER 255) URBAN COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under Sections 2, 5 and 10 of the Urban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255) to be read withSection 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government,do by this order, further amend the order, published in the Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of 10th August, 1978 relating tothe Vavuniya Urban Council as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Vavuniya Urban Council shall consist of such number of members to be elected and returnedin terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i.Twelve members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of the Urban CouncilsOrdinance (Chapter 255) ;

ii.Eight members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of the UrbanCouncils Ordinance (Chapter 255) ;

iii.Such number of members determined by the Commissioner of Elections under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (4) ofSection 65B of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) in terms of paragraph (c) of Sub-section(1) of Section 5 of the Urban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255)”

2. The term of Office of the members of this Urban Council shall commence with effect from 15th February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.Colombo,01st November, 2017.

PRADESHIYA SABHAS ACT, No. 15 OF 1987

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under Sub-section (1) of Section 2,Section 4 and Section 6 of the Pradeshiya Sabhas Act,No. 15 of 1987 to be read with Section 18 of the Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha, Minister ofProvincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, further amend the order, published in the Gazette ExtraordinaryNo. 453/16 of 12th May, 1987 relating to the Vavuniya North Pradeshiya Sabha as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Vavuniya North Pradeshiya Sabha shall consist of such number of members to be elected andreturned in terms of following i, ii and iii Sub paragraphs.

i.Fourteen members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the Pradeshiya SabhasAct, No. 15 of 1987 ;

ii.Nine members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the PradeshiyaSabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987;

iii.Such number of members determined by the Commissioner of Elections under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (4) ofSection 65B of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) in terms of paragraph (b) of Sub-section(1) of Section 4 of the Pradeshiya Sabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987”

2. The term of Office of the members of this Pradeshiya Sabha shall commence with effect from 15th February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.Colombo,01st November, 2017.

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under Sub-section (1) of Section 2, Section 4 and Section 6 of the Pradeshiya SabhasAct, No. 15 of 1987 to be read with Section 18 of the Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha, Minister ofProvincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, further amend the order, which was finally amended by theGazette Extraordinary No. 1687/3 of 03rd January, 2011 and Gazette extraordinary No. 1687/30 of 06th January, 2011,published in the Gazette Extraordinary No. 453/19 of 12th May , 1987 relating to the Akkaraipattu Pradeshiya Sabha asfollows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph:

“(4) Determine that the Akkaraipattu Pradeshiya Sabha shall consist of such number of members to beelected and returned in terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs:

i. Five members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the Pradeshiya SabhasAct, No. 15 of 1987 ;

ii. Three members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of thePradeshiya Sabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987 ;

iii. Such number of members determined by the Commissioner of Elections under paragraph (a) ofSub-section (4) of Section 65B of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) in terms ofparagraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the Pradeshiya Sabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987”

2. The term of office of the members of this Pradeshiya Sabha shall commence with effect from 15th February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,01st November, 2017.

CHAPTER (255) URBAN COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under Sections 2, 5 and 10 of the Urban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255) to be read withSection 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government,do by this order, further amend the order, published in the Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of 10th August, 1978 relating tothe Trincomalee Urban Council as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Trincomalee Urban Council shall consist of such number of members to be electedand returned in terms of following i, ii and iii Sub paragraphs.

i. Fourteen members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of the UrbanCouncils Ordinance (Chapter 255) ;

ii. Nine members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of theUrban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255) ;

iii. Such number of members determined by the Commissioner of Elections under paragraph (a) ofSub-section (4) of Section 65B of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) in terms ofparagraph (c) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of the Urban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255)”

2. The term of office of the members of this Urban Council shall commence with effect from 15th February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,01st November, 2017.

2. The term of office of the members of this Pradeshiya Sabha shall commence with effect from 15th February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,01st November, 2017.

Order

i.Twenty-One members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the PradeshiyaSabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987 ;

ii.Fourteen members to be elected and returned under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of thePradeshiya Sabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987;

iii.Such number of members determined by the Commissioner of Elections under paragraph (a) of Sub-section(4) of Section 65B of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) in terms of paragraph (b) ofSub-section (1) of Section 4 of the Pradeshiya Sabhas Act, No. 15 of 1987”

2. The term of office of the members of this Pradeshiya Sabha shall commence with effect from 15th of February, 2018.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,01st November, 2017.
